Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the clouds space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 976 and the convergence rate is O(698/n^3).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the clouds decomposition. By minimality, the element a^976 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 698-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
